Korea DRAM prices surge as HBM shift deepens global supply shortfall - CHOSUNBIZ - Chosunbiz

<a href="https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiekFVX3lxTE1TSkV3cFVZMThSSW4wdGNmSHdOUnkzTTFlRW5JVXFrQlVscnBnN2lqR3c5QzhDZ0wzcEJoSkRMWVFHSWhJdl9HMGQyaVNyNXNBMWVwWHlrWTlZNnNxQ0tJTXdvQWJaaGFZWnY3X0xDY1VpTVBseXhQajhR0gGOAUFVX3lxTE9hTXJ5OEVMTXE3SWVtUXRTZ3ZXeWREcW9RWVhvTjZXcm9aa2lFaUtJT1FFdlJONXBQRDJJUHNWb2VVLTR2aUhxb2pfd3dmd2drTXloQmFtLUR1SGMzdlpCay1IODVIejJncVcxVTdMWG55NUdBeWZET01pS0ZRX2R1cWh5YVNDTmlCUFhKUnc?oc=5" target="_blank">Korea DRAM prices surge as HBM shift deepens global supply shortfall - CHOSUNBIZ</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<font color="#6f6f6f">Chosunbiz</font>

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
A Korea DRAM price surge is being tied to an HBM-driven global supply shortfall.

内容:
报道称 HBM 转产加深了全球内存供应缺口，韩国 DRAM 价格随之大涨。

Why it matters:
Tight DRAM/HBM supply raises input costs and constrains capacity for AI accelerators, and a memory shortage is a classic capacity-bottleneck signal in the AI-capex cycle rather than a demand-side red flag.

影响:
DRAM/HBM 供给收紧会推高 AI 加速卡的物料成本并限制产能，属于 AI capex 周期里典型的产能瓶颈信号，而不是需求端的利空。

What this means:
News coverage cites analysts projecting a 50% memory-price surge this quarter, with Micron shares rallying on the report.

内容:

Why it matters:

影响:

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
TrendForce projects DRAM and NAND flash will account for 68% of major CSPs' 2027 capex as memory prices surge.

内容:
TrendForce 预测，到 2027 年 DRAM 和 NAND 闪存将占主要云服务商资本开支的 68%，内存价格正在飙升。

Why it matters:
Memory is becoming the binding constraint on AI infrastructure spend rather than an incidental line item — when two-thirds of cloud capex goes to DRAM/NAND, chip supply (not GPU allocation) starts gating how fast AI buildout can scale, and it raises the breakeven bar for every AI workload riding on that hardware.

影响:
内存正在从资本开支里的次要项变成硬约束——当三分之二的云端 capex 都砸进 DRAM/NAND，能不能扩大 AI 部署规模就要看芯片供给而不是 GPU 分配，这也把所有跑在这些硬件上的 AI 应用的盈亏平衡线抬高了。

An IPO timeline signals the current private-funding model (mega rounds from Microsoft/SoftBank/etc.) is being treated as a bridge, not the end state — capital needs are outrunning what private markets alone will keep supplying at this valuation. It's a data point on how OpenAI plans to fund its compute buildout, not a stress signal on its own.

影响:
IPO 时间表说明现在靠 Microsoft、SoftBank 等巨额私募轮撑着的融资模式被当作过渡方案，不是终点——按目前估值，光靠私募资金已经跟不上算力扩张所需的资本量。这条本身是 OpenAI 融资规划的信息，不算独立的压力信号。

Plus, SoftBank plans a record $6.3 billion retail bond sale to raise funds for its investment commitments to OpenAI. 影响:

Alibaba Drops, SoftBank Bond Sale, Bitcoin Rally | Stock Movers

Today's biggest winners and losers in the stock market.

On this episode of Stock Movers:

- Alibaba (BABA) is tumbling after announcing a plan to raise about HK$80 billion ($10.2 billion) from a share sale. The share placement is expected to dilute earnings and hurt stock performance in the near term, analysts said. Meanwhile, the fundraising will help drive AI-led growth and the impact on earnings will be limited if the company can improve AI payoffs, they added. The tech and chip sectors are seeing a broader selloff globally.

- SoftBank (SFTBY) is moving on news it plans a record ¥1 trillion retail bond sale to raise funds for its investment commitments to OpenAI.

- Bitcoin (BTC) is continuing its rally. Spot Bitcoin exchange-traded funds had their strongest weekly inflow in 10 months last week as the original cryptocurrency surged. (Source: Bloomberg)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
Bloomberg's Stock Movers roundup ties three separate moves together: Alibaba (BABA) is falling after unveiling a HK$80B (~$10.2B) share placement to fund AI growth, SoftBank (SFTBY) is planning a record ¥1T retail bond sale to cover its OpenAI investment commitments, and Bitcoin continues rallying on record spot-ETF inflows.

内容:
彭博 Stock Movers 栏目把三条消息放在一起播报：阿里巴巴 (BABA) 因宣布约 HK$80 亿（约 $10.2B）的股票配售用于 AI 增长而股价下跌，软银 (SFTBY) 计划发行创纪录的 ¥1 万亿零售债券以支付对 OpenAI 的投资承诺，比特币则在现货 ETF 创 10 个月最强单周净流入的带动下持续上涨。

Why it matters:
Two of the largest AI-capex backers are turning to dilutive equity (Alibaba) and debt (SoftBank) to keep funding commitments, which is exactly the strain-on-financing pattern the bubble-stress thesis watches for — capex outrunning organic cash generation. The BTC/crypto-inflow angle is unrelated noise bundled into the same segment, not a capex signal.

影响:
两大 AI 资本开支的重要出资方都在转向融资：阿里靠增发股票（会稀释每股收益），软银靠发债，都是为了填补对 AI 的投入缺口——这正是 bubble-stress 论点关注的「资本开支跑赢自身造血能力」的信号。比特币/加密资金流入那段和资本开支无关，只是同一节目里混进来的噪音。

SoftBank Plans Record $6.3 Billion Retail Bond Sale in Japan

SoftBank Group Corp. plans a record ¥1 trillion ($6.3 billion) retail bond sale by any issuer in Japan as the conglomerate raises funds for its investment commitments to OpenAI.

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
SoftBank Group plans a record ¥1 trillion ($6.3B) domestic retail bond sale in Japan to fund its investment commitments to OpenAI.

内容:
软银集团计划在日本发行创纪录的 1 万亿日元（63 亿美元）零售债券，用于兑现对 OpenAI 的投资承诺。

Why it matters:
SoftBank is tapping retail debt markets — not cash on hand — to meet OpenAI funding obligations, pushing capital-raising risk onto Japanese retail bondholders. A record-size issuance signals the scale of committed capital is straining even SoftBank's balance sheet, reinforcing the thesis that OpenAI's capex needs are outrunning what conventional financing can absorb.

影响:
软银这次是靠零售债市场融资，不是动用自有现金，把筹资风险转嫁给了日本散户债权人。发行规模创纪录，说明对 OpenAI 的承诺资金规模已经大到连软银自己的资产负债表都吃紧，进一步印证 OpenAI 的资本开支需求正在超出常规融资渠道的承受能力。

What this means:
A news item reports enterprise SSDs now cost 18.6x more than HDDs, with 30TB drives hitting $22,600, and hard-drive supply sold out through 2027.

内容:
一则新闻称，企业级 SSD 价格已是 HDD 的 18.6 倍，30TB 硬盘报价 $22,600，硬盘供应缺货将持续到 2027 年。

Why it matters:
Sustained multi-year HDD shortage plus SSD price premium signals real physical capacity constraints in storage supply chains feeding AI datacenter buildouts, not just a transient spike — a structural cost tailwind for capex and a hint that hyperscaler storage budgets are being squeezed by scarcity, not demand softness.

影响:
硬盘长期缺货加上 SSD 价格溢价，说明存储供应链的物理产能瓶颈是结构性的，不是短期波动，这会持续推高 AI 数据中心建设的资本开支，也说明超大规模厂商的存储预算压力来自供给短缺而非需求走弱。

内容:
SpaceX 股价在一次计划内解禁前后下跌 4%，同时期权市场出现 $160M 的卖出看跌期权（put-selling），报道认为这是看涨信号。

Open Machine CEO on Anthropic, OpenAI IPO Potential

Allie Miller, CEO of Open Machine, shared insights on how top technology companies are managing their AI investments to balance cost and return. She explained that frontier companies typically divide their AI efforts into two groups: one focused on raising the baseline capabilities across departments like finance, legal, marketing, and sales with modest subscription-level spending, and a second, smaller 'frontier' team that operates with significantly higher budgets and greater experimentation freedom. She speaks with Romaine Bostick on "The Close." (Source: Bloomberg)

内容:

Why it matters:

目标价上调会直接抬高二级市场对 SpaceX 上市前估值的定价基准,也会影响指数纳入相关的测算——这类卖方叙事容易在没有新披露财务数据支撑的情况下，提前透支被动资金流入的预期。

Real yields near cycle highs raise the discount rate on long-duration AI capex bets and make debt-funded data-center buildouts more expensive to carry — a direct headwind to the AI-infrastructure spend thesis.

影响:
实际利率逼近周期高位，会提高长久期 AI capex 项目的贴现率，也让数据中心那类举债扩张的融资成本更高——直接利空 AI 基建的支出逻辑。
